# Locale inventory (OKR KR1.1)

Generated by `node tools/check-locale.mjs --baseline` (https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/tools/check-locale.mjs) from the working tree on top of commit 3a569f48. Do not edit by hand.
The hand-written notes (what each category means, which adapter takes it, the first moves) are in
https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/docs/planet-architecture.md , "Locale inventory: notes".

Rule of counting: one per line of code (comments removed) that matches the category. A line can count in more than one
category. The region configs, the adapters and their tests (`docklands/geo/regions/`, `docklands/geo/adapters/`,
`docklands/geo/test/`) are where these constants belong and are not counted. `npm run check` fails when a count goes up.

## Counts

| category | what | lines | files | belongs in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bng` | British National Grid (EPSG:27700) projection, names and calls | 168 | 33 | uk-os (UK adapter: BNG <-> WGS84 through OSTN15; core works in WGS84 / ECEF / ENU) |
| `ostn15` | OSTN15 / OSGM15 grid transformation | 41 | 24 | uk-os |
| `odn` | Ordnance Datum Newlyn heights (m OD) | 140 | 47 | uk-os (heightDatum of the region; core heights are ellipsoidal or region datum by config) |
| `origin` | the model origin E 537,550 N 180,300 (One Canada Square) and its uses | 99 | 38 | region config docklands (origin {lon,lat,h}; the BNG model frame is a fixed transform of its ENU) |
| `box` | the model box and its importers | 56 | 27 | region config docklands (bbox; tools take --region) |
| `timezone` | London time zone and London-time functions | 98 | 32 | region timeZone (docklands/geo/clock.js) |
| `zone_latlon` | latitude literals of the zone (51.3 to 51.6), e.g. Open-Meteo, adsb.lol, METAR calls | 113 | 56 | region origin / bbox, or the place in view (KR5.3) |
| `uk_sources` | UK-only sources hard-wired (hosts under .uk, postcodes.io, the EGLC METAR) | 363 | 60 | source adapters with area and licence class (KR1.4) |
| `ui_en_gb` | 'en-GB' formats in the user interface | 87 | 40 | message catalogue and the visitor locale (Intl, KR1.3) |
| all | | 1165 | 161 | |
